## 2026-10-17 - Hephaestus run 39021488755 (owner "Why halt?" on #582: model-retirement outage post-mortem, native per-OS proof dispatched)

State snapshot at survey: open PRs: none. Open issues: #582 (Rust-migration parent, Phases 1-7 + Final merged Refs-only; Windows/macOS native parity rows open), #552 (Pac-Man, OPEN awaiting owner live-terminal confirmation; migration gameplay reference), #70 lab-health (carries today's crash escalations), #42 brainstorm standing only. Trigger allowlist re-verified this run: 19 entries in maintainer.yml `workflows:` vs 19 live non-maintainer workflow `name:` fields, exact set match, no drift. Main tip: 16c00979 (lab model-switch commit, authored 09:31:00Z, pushed 09:31:37Z).

Trigger: owner comment "Why halt?" on #582 at 09:31:54Z (issue_comment created; run https://github.com/Userfrom1995/RandomLabs/actions/runs/39021488755). Notification payload rendered briefly, delivery here was issue_comment on #582.

Findings (root cause of the apparent halt): (1) Provider-side retirement of the pinned free model `opencode/muse-spark-1.3-contributor-free`. From ~03:25Z every agent arm crashed instantly with `Model not found: opencode/muse-spark-1.3-contributor-free`. Timeline reconstructed from live runs and #70 comments: auditor schedule arm crashed -> bounded self-heal re-dispatch crashed the same way -> cap reached, escalation posted on #70 at 03:25:05Z ("Manual triage required. /oc maintainer"); curator schedule arm same shape at 03:28:40Z; maintainer arms 38998699650, 38998723579, 38998924889, 38998945031 posted "Model not found" bot comments linking themselves; scheduled maintainer ticks 38999770178 (03:41:49Z) and 39017154198 (08:23:05Z) died ~25s in, hollow-green with no decision file and no log entry. Today's log stub (above) records 8 silent-run markers: this is the exact silent-stall class the prompt warns about, caused by a dead model, not by timeout. (2) Recovery: commit 16c00979 (owner-authored under the chicken-and-egg case: lab.yml itself was pinned to the dead model so no lab arm could apply the fix) re-pinned all 20 workflow `model:` sites, maintainer.yml `MODEL` env, and opencode.json `model` + `small_model` to `opencode/deepseek-v4.1-flash-free`, and added `variant: max` at all 20 composite call sites. Verified: zero `muse-spark` references remain in `.github/` or `opencode.json`; 20 model pins vs 20 `variant: max` sites; only historical references remain in ideas/, progress/, archive/ (records, not live pins). This run (39021488755) is the first maintainer execution on the new pin: live proof of recovery. (3) Why no dispatch had started on #582 after the Final phase: by design. The Final PR body reads Refs #582 and docs/parity.md leaves exactly two rows open: Windows/macOS per-layer E2E runs plus packaged artifacts (zip, .app) never executed on native OSes. No Linux runner can sign those rows; the standing playbook required a natively-proved vehicle or an owner order. The owner's "Why halt?" is the resume signal, and the per-OS tester arms are the vehicle the open question named.

Decisions + rationale: `[{"action": "ping", "target": 70, ...}, {"action": "auditor", "issue": 70}, {"action": "test-macos", "issue": 582}, {"action": "test-windows", "issue": 582}]`. (a) ping on #70: triage note closing the self-heal escalations with the root cause and the landed fix; the board's "manual triage required" comments now have their resolution on-thread. (b) auditor on #70: fresh health sweep on the recovered pins, run on the new model; the last auditor attempt crashed ~6h ago, so this is triage, not spam. (c) test-macos + test-windows on #582: peros-test resolves head_ref=main (no open `opencode/issue582-*` PR), so the testers build and drive the real layers natively on macOS and Windows runners against main, stage the packaged artifacts, and record OS evidence; their reports either flip the parity rows or hand us native defect evidence. Two entries on one issue is a deliberate reading of the one-entry-per-target rule: the hardcoded dedupe unit is (action, target), the two actions are distinct independent gate arms with separate concurrency groups, and both are required to close the two distinct open rows. macOS comment posts first, so its run takes the workflow-level concurrency slot and the Windows run queues behind it (cancel-in-progress: false), both land. (d) No `lab` dispatch: the only infra need (model repin) is already live and verified; no unaddressed failure class remains.

Callbacks: public answer comment on #582 (comment.md); ping posted on #70; hardcoded PAT step posts `/oc auditor` on #70 and `/oc test-macos` + `/oc test-windows` on #582. No other triggers posted.

Next run: read the two per-OS reports on #582. Both clean -> verify the parity/scoreboard rows get flipped by the testers' committed evidence or dispatch the docs flip, then plan the natively-proved close of #582 and the #552 retirement step (owner live-terminal confirmation still the other open item on #552). Any native failure -> Fixer with the exact native repro, then fresh gate. Model watch: deepseek-v4.1-flash-free is the live pin; if it dies or rate-limits, route the lab model-fallback ladder (never self-edit workflows). Carried hardening residuals stay queued for a future vehicle. Standby otherwise.

Open questions: (1) Can the hosted macOS/Windows runners drive the GUI probe heads, or will the testers honestly report headless E2E + packaging only? (2) Does flipping parity.md rows happen via tester commits on an issue (they only commit on PRs) or via a small follow-up docs phase? (3) Owner live-terminal confirmation on #552 still outstanding.

## Run 39030405503 - trigger: `/oc maintainer` on #593 (Tester handoff) at 11:53:24Z - completed ~12:08Z

**Outcome snapshot (survey ~11:54-12:07Z):** #593 MERGED 12:04:28Z -> main `6ea4f956` by a concurrent maintainer session's owner-credential PAT sweep (my own sweep would have found it already closed; the PR body carried `Closes #591`, and #591 auto-closed at 12:05:49Z). #599 merged 11:56:48Z -> main `3da1d7d1`, #596 closed 11:57:23Z. #592: review round 2 verdict 12:01:26Z carried exactly one blocking item (rebase + resolve only `maze-chase/docs/scoreboard.md`, keep both intents); fix run 39030910538 in flight since 12:01:32Z. Windows arm 39026429150 still in its agent step (started 11:06:53Z, ~60 min in). Sweeps clean: zero failure/timed_out/startup_failure in the last 100 runs, zero held, allowlist 19/19 exact set match.

**THE INCIDENT - shallow session workspace, false-orphan merge skip (the durable lesson):**
- My session's workspace arrived SHALLOW despite `actions/checkout fetch-depth: 0`: `.git/shallow` (41 bytes, 1 entry) with mtime 11:53Z, created during the opencode CLI session bootstrap BEFORE any of my commands (my first fetch ~11:55Z). Verified via `git rev-parse --is-shallow-repository` = `true`.
- In that state `git merge-base origin/main 49e91814` returned EMPTY even though 49e91814 is a legitimate rebase whose true merge-base with main is `9cbc8f5c` (confirmed only after `git fetch --unshallow`). Plain `git fetch origin main`, `git fetch origin <branch>`, and `git fetch origin <sha>` do NOT deepen a depth-1 boundary; only `--unshallow` restores connectivity.
- Consequence if unhandled: the PAT merge sweep (`maintainer.yml:803`, merge-base checks at 835/840) reads every workflow-touching PR as an orphan on a shallow workspace and SILENTLY skips the merge behind the benign echo "no common ancestor with main (orphan); skipping direct merge, requires re-link" - no error, no red run. The end-of-job integrity guard (910-914) already carries a defensive unshallow for exactly this class; the merge step does not.
- Remediation in-session: `git fetch --unshallow origin` in the workspace before the sweep step. Controlled reproduction in /tmp at ~12:02Z confirmed the exact mechanism end-to-end (clone -> depth-1 fetch -> shallow=true -> plain fetches do not deepen -> merge-base EMPTY -> --unshallow -> 9cbc8f5c).
- Filed as a `create_issue` decision this run (title: "[Infra] PAT merge sweep silently skips a legitimately linked PR when the run workspace is shallow (no --unshallow guard)"), with the full reproduction, evidence, candidate one-line guard, and the open question (trigger conditions unpinned: #595's PAT merge at 10:50Z on the same CLI v1.19.3 succeeded). Untriaged-by-design note in STATE: route `lab` once #601's PR is not mid-flight on `silent-stall-audit.sh`.

**Decisions + rationale:**
1. `{"action": "lab", "issue": 601}` - the displacement fix. Hold condition satisfied: #593 merged at 12:04:28Z and #601's scope (opencode.yml, lab.yml, opencode-*.yml, silent-stall-audit.sh, AGENTS.md) no longer races any open PR. The concurrent fix-handoff session (39030613875) correctly declined to dispatch it (its decisions were `[]`).
2. `{"action": "create_issue", ...}` - the shallow-workspace hazard (above). The issue-filing test held: observed on a live run, mechanism reproduced under control, fix surgical and modeled on an existing guard.
3. No merge action from my run: #593 was merged by another session's sweep before mine would run; no other PR is gate-clear. No comment/dispatch on #592: the fix loop owns it (39030910538) and review round 3 re-triggers on the fixer's push.

**Callbacks / acknowledgements:** none owed. Run trigger was the Tester handoff on #593 (`/oc approve-test` 11:53:22Z) - gate cleared, merge landed. The two `/oc maintainer` triggers at 11:54:25Z (#599) and 11:56:52Z (#592) were both owned by concurrent sessions that completed at 12:04:41Z; no duplicate work.

**Follow-through on my own comments:** comment.md records the merge, the now-in-force ladder, the #601 dispatch, and the shallow-workspace disclosure on #593. Personality unchanged (empty).

**Evidence links:** run https://github.com/Userfrom1995/RandomLabs/actions/runs/39030405503; #593 merge 12:04:28Z (mergedBy owner PAT); main `6ea4f956` = the ladder series rebased (tip commit "lab: record the third-round terminal-anchoring decision and delegation ledger (Refs #591)").

**For future self:** if a workflow-touching PR shows MERGEABLE + fresh approve-test and still sits open across two ticks, FIRST check `git rev-parse --is-shallow-repository` and `git merge-base origin/main <head>` in a fresh shell - a shallow workspace makes the sweep skip silently. Until the shallow issue's guard lands, unshallow manually before relying on the sweep.

 - Hephaestus, the Maintainer
